  onEnemyKilled(key: string, enemy?: Enemy) {
    // Boss 击败记账已由 Boss 逻辑块通用化(downed_<vanillaId>)
    void key;
    // 史莱姆雨击杀推进（NPC.cs:79883-79898：slimeRainNPC 击杀 → killCount≥150 且场上无王 → 召王）
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null && this.world.weather.slimeRain) {
      if (this.world.weather.advanceSlimeRainKill(enemy.vanillaId) && !this.boss) {
        this.summonBoss('king_slime');
      }
    }
    // 月事件计分（NPC.cs CheckProgressFrostMoon/PumpkinMoon :79245/:79487）：
    // 必须在掉落结算之后同帧调用（Enemy.hurt 尾部 onEnemyKilled 即 NPCLoot 之后，
    // 对齐原版 checkDead NPCLoot → CheckProgress 顺序 NPC.cs:79020-79021），
    // 保证本波掉落 gate 用的是进波前的 wave。进波公告由此处广播
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null && moonEventActive(this.world)) {
      const diff = this.player.appearance?.difficulty ?? 0;
      const ann = addMoonEventKill(this.world, enemy.vanillaId, diff >= 2, diff >= 3);
      if (ann) this.newText(invasionWaveText(ann), 175, 75, 255);
    }
    // 星璇塔事件（NPC.cs:80080-80146）：
    //  本组专属怪死亡 → 发射 629 TowerDamageBolt → 命中塔盾 -1（归属表 LunarEvent.MINION_TOWER）；
    //  塔真死（180t 死亡演出后）→ downedTower_* + UpdateLunarApocalypse + MessageLunarApocalypse。
    //  与月事件计分同位（掉落结算之后同帧，对齐原版 checkDead 尾段顺序）
    if (enemy && enemy.vanillaId != null) {
      if (isTowerId(enemy.vanillaId)) {
        this.onLunarTowerKilled(enemy.vanillaId);
      } else if (this.world.lunarEvent.active && isTowerMinion(enemy.vanillaId)) {
        this.onLunarMinionKilled(enemy);
      }
    }
    // 入侵击杀扣分（NPC.cs:79021-79054：组号匹配当前入侵类型才计分，加权扣减）
    if (!enemy || this.world.invasionType <= 0 || enemy.vanillaId == null) return;
    const group = INVASION_GROUP[enemy.vanillaId];
    if (!group || group !== this.world.invasionType) return;
    this.world.invasionSize = Math.max(0, this.world.invasionSize - (KILL_WEIGHT[enemy.vanillaId] ?? 1));
  }

  // ================= 星璇塔 / 天界入侵（LunarEvent.ts 宿主回调侧） =================

  /** LunarHost：造塔/广播/清教徒（LunarEvent 保持纯逻辑，不反向依赖 Game） */
  private lunarHost(): LunarHost {
    return {
      spawnTowerNpc: (id, tx, ty) => this.spawnLunarTower(id, tx, ty),
      broadcast: (text, r, g, b) => this.newText(text, r, g, b),
      clearCultists: () => {
        // GetRidOfCultists（WorldGen.cs:87508-87521：移除 437/438/379）
        for (const e of this.entities.enemies) {
          const en = e as Enemy;
          if (!en.dead && (en.vanillaId === 437 || en.vanillaId === 438 || en.vanillaId === 379)) {
            en.dead = true;
          }
        }
      },
    };
  }

  /** 塔就位（WorldGen.TriggerLunarApocalypse 内 NewNPC 语义：锚点 = 列中心 + 底部贴格顶） */
  private spawnLunarTower(id: number, tx: number, ty: number): void {
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE);
    if (!e) return;
    e.x = tx * TILE + 8 - e.w / 2;
    e.y = ty * TILE - e.h;
    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
  }

  /** TriggerLunarApocalypse（WorldGen.cs:87371-87436：教徒死调用，见 Boss 逻辑块） */
  triggerLunarApocalypse(): void {
    triggerLunarApocalypse(this.world, new RNG((Math.random() * 1e9) | 0), this.lunarHost());
  }

  /** 场上是否存在存活 NPC id（UpdateLunarApocalypse 的塔/月总在场探测用） */
  private hasNpcId(id: number): boolean {
    return this.entities.enemies.some((e) => {
      const en = e as Enemy;
      return !en.dead && en.vanillaId === id;
    });
  }

  /** 塔真死（NPC.cs:80122-80146：downedTower_* + Update/MessageLunarApocalypse） */
  private onLunarTowerKilled(towerId: number): void {
    onTowerKilled(this.world, towerId, (id) => this.hasNpcId(id), this.lunarHost());
  }

  /** 本组怪死亡 → 朝对应塔发射 629 TowerDamageBolt 真弹体（NPC.cs:80089-80121 发弹 +
   *  Projectile.cs:69784-69819 命中结算）：弹体 5px/t 追塔 + 红尘尾（AI_122 :33747-33778），
   *  命中才扣盾 1；扣盾前盾非 0 → 塔 ai[3]=1 闪光 120t（:69791 等）；盾破瞬间
   *  NPCDeath58（NPC.cs:41148-41151 的 flag99 翻转支） */
  private onLunarMinionKilled(enemy: Enemy): void {
    const towerId = towerBoltTarget(this.world, enemy.vanillaId!);
    if (towerId == null) return;
    // FindFirstNPC(塔) 场上无塔时原版 ai[0]=-1 → 弹体首帧自灭（AI_122 :33753-33761）
    const tower = (this.entities.enemies as Enemy[]).find(
      (e) => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === towerId);
    if (!tower) return;
    const findTower = () => (this.entities.enemies as Enemy[]).find(
      (e) => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === towerId) ?? null;
    this.entities.add(new TowerBolt(enemy.cx, enemy.cy, findTower, (t) => {
      const res = onTowerBoltHit(this.world, towerId);
      if (res.flash) t.ai3 = 1;  // 盾非 0 时塔 ai[3]=1（闪光 120t）
      if (res.shieldZero) this.sfx.play('roar', 0.8, t.cx, t.cy);

    return { img, sx: 0, sy: idx * m.frameH, sw: m.frameW, sh: m.frameH };

  /** 原版 tile/item 英文名（全量表，未白名单的也有） */
  vTileName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.tileNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }
  vItemName(id: number): string | null { return this.vanilla.itemNames?.[String(id)] ?? null; }

  vnpcMeta(npcId: number): VanillaNpcMeta | null {
    return this.vanilla.npcs?.[String(npcId)] ?? null;
  }

  /** 原版杂项单图（呼吸气泡等） */
  vmisc(path: string): DrawRect | null {
    const img = this.vimages.get(path);
    if (!img) return null;
    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
  }

  /** UI 贴图（vanilla-ui/ 命名空间）。name 为白名单键，如 'UI_PanelBackground'。
   *  按需懒加载(消费方每帧重查,未就绪返回 null 自兜底) */
  vui(name: string): DrawRect | null {
    const path = this.uiFiles[name];
    if (!path) return null;
    const img = this.ensureUiImage(path);
    if (!img) return null;
    return { img, sx: 0, sy: 0, sw: img.width, sh: img.height };
  }

  private _uiPending = new Set<string>();
  private ensureUiImage(file: string): HTMLImageElement | null {
    const hit = this.uiimages.get(file);
    if (hit) return hit;
    if (this._uiPending.has(file)) return null;
    this._uiPending.add(file);
    const im = new Image();
    im.onload = () => { this.uiimages.set(file, im); this._uiPending.delete(file); };
    im.onerror = () => { this._uiPending.delete(file); };
    im.src = `sprites/${encodeURI(file)}`;
    return null;
  }

  /** 原版物品图标。构建期已 shelf-pack 进少数 Item_Atlas_k.png 图集(ix/iy/iw/ih

  vicon(itemId: number): DrawRect | null {

      return { img, sx: m.ix, sy: m.iy ?? 0, sw: m.iw, sh: m.ih };

function checkArenaScore(
  game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number,
): { left: { x: number; y: number }; right: { x: number; y: number } } {
  const maxDist = 100; // Main.MaxWorldViewSize.X/16 的常见档（SendWalker 默认 100）
  const ptx = Math.floor(cx / TILE), pty = Math.floor(cy / TILE);
  const [, floorY] = expandVertically(game, ptx, pty, 0, 4);
  const start = { x: ptx, y: floorY };
  const l = sendWalker(game, start, 10, -1, maxDist);
  const r = sendWalker(game, start, 10, 1, maxDist);
  return { left: { x: l.x + 1, y: l.y }, right: { x: r.x - 1, y: r.y } };
}

function findCrystal(game: GameHooks): Enemy | null {
  for (const n of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
    if (!n.dead && n.vanillaId === 548) return n;
  }
  return null;

  }

  /** 商店上架门（Chest.SetupShop if 门 → 本作状态映射；未实现事件门不上架） */
  private shopCondOk(cond: string | null): boolean {
    if (!cond) return true;
    const f = this.world.flags;
    switch (cond) {
      case 'hardMode': return !!f.hardMode;
      case 'downedBoss1': return !!(f['downed_4'] || f.downedEyeOfCthulhu);
      case 'downedBoss2': return !!(f['downed_13'] || f['downed_266']);
      case 'downedBoss3': return !!(f['downed_35'] || f.downedSkeletron);
      case 'downedMechBossAny':
        return !!(f['downed_125'] || f['downed_126'] || f['downed_127'] || f['downed_134']);

      // 分档需重提数据（依赖登记：extract-shops.mjs 捕获 case 相位 + JSON 再生）
      case 'moonPhase': return true;
      case 'night': return !this.world.clock.isDay;
      case 'day': return this.world.clock.isDay;
      case 'zoneSnow': return !!this.scene?.zoneSnow;
      case 'zoneJungle': return !!this.scene?.zoneJungle;
      case 'zoneGraveyard': return !!this.scene?.zoneGraveyard;
      case 'zoneMeteor': return !!this.scene?.zoneMeteor;
      case 'zoneUnderworld': return !!this.scene?.zoneUnderworldHeight;
      case 'crimson': return this.world.crimson;
      case 'bloodMoon': return this.world.clock.bloodMoon;
      case 'xMas': return this.world.clock.xMas;

---
// l10n 键完整性审计核心（供 vite 插件 / vitest / CLI 共用）。
// 提取 src 全部 Lang.text/Lang.has 字面键，对语言包解析（LanguageManager.getTextValue
// 同语义：点路径逐段下行 + 整段键兜底）。
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';

/** 收集目录下全部 .ts 文件（递归） */
function collectTs(dir, out = []) {
  for (const e of readdirSync(dir)) {
    const p = join(dir, e);
    if (statSync(p).isDirectory()) collectTs(p, out);
    else if (/\.tsx?$/.test(e)) out.push(p);
  }
  return out;
}

/** 点路径解析：逐段下行，剩余段按整段键兜底（扁平合并键如 Mods."SandboxWorld.NPC.X"） */
export function resolvePackKey(tree, path) {
  const segs = path.split('.');
  let cur = tree;
  for (let i = 0; i < segs.length; i++) {
    const key = segs.slice(i).join('.');
    if (typeof cur === 'object' && cur !== null && key in cur) return cur[key];
    if (typeof cur !== 'object' || cur === null || !(segs[i] in cur)) return undefined;
    cur = cur[segs[i]];
  }
  return cur;
}

/** 审计：src 全部 Lang.text/has('字面键') + 所有 Mods.SandboxWorld.* 字面串
 *  （含经 labelKey/descKey 等间接传递后仍走 Lang.text 的自定义键）是否都在 pack 命中 */
export function auditL10n(srcDir, packPath) {
  const pack = JSON.parse(readFileSync(packPath, 'utf8'));
  const used = new Map(); // key → [file:line]
  const KEY_RE = /Lang\.(?:text|has)\(\s*['"]([A-Za-z0-9_.\-]+)['"]/g;
  const CUSTOM_RE = /['"](Mods\.SandboxWorld\.[A-Za-z0-9_.\-]+)['"]/g;
  for (const f of collectTs(srcDir)) {
    const lines = readFileSync(f, 'utf8').split('\n');
    lines.forEach((line, i) => {
      for (const re of [KEY_RE, CUSTOM_RE]) {
        for (const m of line.matchAll(re)) {
          if (!used.has(m[1])) used.set(m[1], []);
          used.get(m[1]).push(`${f}:${i + 1}`);
        }
      }
    });
  }
  const missing = [];
  for (const [key, locs] of [...used.entries()].sort()) {
    if (resolvePackKey(pack, key) === undefined) missing.push({ key, locs: locs.slice(0, 2) });

	public static int GetAvailableAmountOfNPCsToSpawnUpToSlot(int amountWeWant, int highestNPCSlotIndexWeWillPick = 100)
	{
		if (amountWeWant <= 0)
		{
			return 0;
		}
		int num = 0;
		for (int i = 0; i < highestNPCSlotIndexWeWillPick; i++)
		{
			if (!Main.npc[i].active)
			{
				num++;
				if (num >= amountWeWant)
				{
					return amountWeWant;
				}
			}
		}
		return num;
	}

	public static IEntitySource GetBossSpawnSource(int targetPlayerIndex)
	{
		return new EntitySource_BossSpawn(Main.player[targetPlayerIndex]);
	}

	public static void SpawnBoss(int spawnPositionX, int spawnPositionY, int Type, int targetPlayerIndex, float ai0 = 0f, float ai1 = 0f, float ai2 = 0f, float ai3 = 0f)
	{
		int maxNPCs = Main.maxNPCs;
		bool flag = Type == 134 || Type == 127 || Type == 126 || Type == 125;
		if (flag)
		{
			AchievementsHelper.MechaMayhem_Clear();
		}
		if (Type == 127 && mechQueen != -1)
		{
			maxNPCs = (mechQueen = NewNPC(GetBossSpawnSource(targetPlayerIndex), spawnPositionX, spawnPositionY, Type, 100, ai0, ai1, ai2, ai3, targetPlayerIndex));
			Main.npc[mechQueen].ai[3] = mechQueen;
		}
		else
		{
			maxNPCs = NewNPC(GetBossSpawnSource(targetPlayerIndex), spawnPositionX, spawnPositionY, Type, 1, ai0, ai1, ai2, ai3, targetPlayerIndex);
		}
		if (maxNPCs == Main.maxNPCs)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (flag)
		{
			AchievementsHelper.MechaMayhem_Start();
		}
		Main.npc[maxNPCs].timeLeft *= 20;
		_ = Main.npc[maxNPCs].TypeName;
		if (Main.netMode == 2 && maxNPCs < Main.maxNPCs)
		{
			NetMessage.SendData(23, -1, -1, null, maxNPCs);
		}
		if (Type == 127 && mechQueen == maxNPCs)
		{
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(Lang.misc[107].ToNetworkText(), ChatColors.BossOrEvent);
			return;
		}
		switch (Type)
		{
		case 125:
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(Lang.misc[48].ToNetworkText(), ChatColors.BossOrEvent);
			break;
		default:
			ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(NetworkText.FromKey("Announcement.HasAwoken", Main.npc[maxNPCs].GetTypeNetName()), ChatColors.BossOrEvent);
			break;
		case 50:
		case 82:
		case 126:
		case 316:
		case 398:
		case 551:
		case 662:
			break;
		}
	}

	private static int Difficulty_3_GetRequiredWaveKills(ref int waveNumber, ref int currentKillCount, bool currentlyInCheckProgress)
	{
		switch (waveNumber)
		{
		case -1:
			return 0;
		case 1:
			return 60;
		case 2:
			return 80;
		case 3:
			return 100;
		case 4:
			return 120;
		case 5:
			return 140;
		case 6:
			return 180;
		case 7:
		{
			int num = NPC.FindFirstNPC(551);
			if (num == -1)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			currentKillCount = 100 - (int)((float)Main.npc[num].life / (float)Main.npc[num].lifeMax * 100f);
			return 100;
		}
		case 8:
			waveNumber = 7;
			currentKillCount = 1;
			if (currentlyInCheckProgress)
			{
				StartVictoryScene();
			}
			return 1;
		default:
			return 10;
		}
	}

	private static int Difficulty_3_GetMonsterPointsWorth(int slainMonsterID)
	{
		if (NPC.waveNumber == 7)
		{
			if (slainMonsterID == 551)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			return 0;
		}
		if ((uint)(slainMonsterID - 551) <= 14u || (uint)(slainMonsterID - 568) <= 10u)
		{
			if (!Main.expertMode)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			return 2;
		}
		return 0;
	}

function item(d: Partial<ItemDef> & { key: string; name: string }) {
  byKey[d.key] = ITEM_DEFS.length;
  ITEM_DEFS.push({ maxStack: 999, ...d } as ItemDef);
}

(p.y + p.h > y1) y1 = p.y + p.h;\n  }\n  x0 -= 800; y0 -= 800; x1 += 800; y1 += 800;\n  st.arena = { x: x0, y: y0, w: x1 - x0, h: y1 - y0 };\n}\n\nexport function shouldBlockBuilding(st: OldOnesArmyState, wx: number, wy: number): boolean {\n  const a = st.arena;\n  return st.ongoing && wx >= a.x && wx <= a.x + a.w && wy >= a.y && wy <= a.y + a.h;\n}\n\n// */\nexport function shouldDropCrystals(st: OldOnesArmyState, betsyLifeRatio: number | null): boolean {\n  const s = invasionStatus(st, betsyLifeRatio);\n  if (st.crystalsLastWave < s.wave) {\n  "new_string": "/** CheckProgress（:344-470）。击杀结算后调用（同帧掉落之后，对齐原版顺序）。 */\nexport function checkProgress(\n  st: OldOnesArmyState, slainId: number, expert: boolean,\n  betsyLifeRatio: number | null,\n): DD2ProgressResult {\n  const res: DD2ProgressResult = { waveComplete: null, victoryScene: false, medals: 0 };\n  if (!st.ongoing || st.lostThisRun || st.wonThisRun || st.timeLeftUntilSpawningBegins !== 0) return res;\n  const status = invasionStatus(st, betsyLifeRatio);\n  const pts = pointsWorth(st, slainId, expert);\n  st.waveKills += pts;\n  st.totalInvasionPoints += pts;\n  if (status.required !== 0 && st.waveKills >= status.required) {\n    st.waveKills = 0;\n    st.waveNumber++;\n    const after = invasionStatus(st, betsyLifeRatio, true);\n    if (after.victoryScene) {\n      // StartVictoryScene（:481-507 经 GetInvasionStatus inCheck 分支触发）→ WonThisRun\n      res.victoryScene = true;\n      st.wonThisRun = true;\n    }\n    if (st.wonThisRun) return res;    // :362-379 已胜利 → 进度同步后返回（不播 WaveComplete）\n    const doneWave = after.wave;      // num3 = 进波后的新波号（:384）\n    res.waveComplete = doneWave === 2\n      ? y: number } | null {\n  const st = world.store;\n  const tileId = TILE_BY_KEY['v_597_pylons'];\n  if (!tileId || !st.inBounds(x, y) || st.get(x, y) !== tileId) return null;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  return {\n    x: x - Math.floor(st.frameX[i] / 18) % 3,\n    y: y - Math.floor(st.frameY[i] / 18) % 4,\n  };\n}\n\n/** IsPlayerNearAPylon（:209-212）：±60 格（TileReachCheckSettings.Pylons）内有 597 */\nexport function isPlayerNearPylon(\n  pylons: TeleportPylonInfo[], playerTileX: number, playerTileY: number,\n): boolean {\n  return pylons.some((p) =>\n    Math.abs(p.x - playerTileX) <= 60 + 1 && Math.abs(p.y - playerTileY) <= 60 + 2);\n}\n\n/** HowManyNPCsDoesPylonNeed（:314-323）：Victory 塔 0，其余 2 */\nexport function npcsNeeded(type: number): number {\n  return type === PYLON.Victory ? 0 : 2;\n}\n\nexport interface NpcHomeLike { homeX: number;   // ZoneScanSize 169×124（中心对齐 scanScene 采样窗）\n  for (const n of npcs) {\n    if (Math.abs(n.homeX - centerX) > halfW || Math.abs(n.homeY - centerY) > halfH) continue;\n    const d = Math.hypot(n.homeX - n.x, n.homeY - n.y);\n    if (d < 100) {\n      left--;\n      if (left === 0) return true;\n    }\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** 蜥蜴神庙早入门禁输入：目标塔格是否神庙墙 87 且在 worldSurface 以下 */\nexport function isInLihzahrdTempleEarly(world: World, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  const st = world.store;\n  if (y <= world.groundLevel) return false;\n  return st.inBounds(x, y) && st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] === 87;\n}\n\n/** DoesPylonAcceptTeleportation（:254-312）：按塔型的生物群系/深度要求。\n *  scene = 塔位 scanScene 结果（Zone 判定即原版 EnoughTilesFor* 阈值判定）；\n *  remixWorld 分支不适用（本作无颠倒世界）。Shimmer 型用微光腔距离近似\n *  （原版 EnoughTilesForShimmer 为 tile 计数阈值，SceneFlags 无该计数——等价边界）。 */\nexport function pylonAcceptsTeleportation(\n  type: number, world: World, scene: SceneFlags, x: number, y: number,\n): boolean {\n  const w = world.store.w;\n  const nearEdge = x >= w - 380 || x <= 380;   // Beach/纯净共用 380 门（:269/:289）\n  switch (type) {\n    case PYLON.SurfacePurity: {\n      // :260-278：地表 + 非边缘 + 无任何主要群系 tile 达标\n      const surface = y <= world.groundLevel;\n      if (!surface || nearEdge) return false;\n      return !(scene.zoneJungle || scene.zoneSnow || scene.zoneDesert\n        || scene.zoneGlowshroom || scene.zoneHallow || scene.zoneCrimson || scene.zoneCorrupt);\n    }\n    case PYLON.Jungle:\n      return scene.zoneJungle;                        // :280\n    case PYLON.Hallow:\n      return scene.zoneHallow;                        // :296\n    case PYLON.Underground:\n      return y >= world.groundLevel;                  // :298（worldSurface 含）\n    case PYLON.Beach: {\n      // :284-294：地表浅层（worldSurface*0.35 < y ≤ worldSurface）+ 世界边缘 380\n      const depthOk = y <= world.groundLevel && y > world.groundLevel * 0.3499999940395355;\n      return nearEdge && depthOk;\n    }\n    case PYLON.Desert:\n      return scene.zoneDesert;                        // :282\n    case PYLON.Snow:\n      return scene.zoneSnow;                          // :278→case Snow\n    case PYLON.GlowingMushroom:\n      return scene.zoneGlowshroom;                    // :288（remix 深度门不适用）\n    case PYLON.Victory:\n      return true;                                    // :308\n  }\n}\n\n/** 门禁判定结果（HandleTeleportRequest :100-205）。fail = Net.* 文案键，null = 通过 */\nexport interface PylonTeleportCheck {\n  ok: boolean;\n  fail: string | null;\n}\n\nexport interface PylonGateInput {\n  /** 玩家 tile 坐标（position.X/16 取整） */\n  playerTileX: number; playerTileY: number;\n  /** 目标塔 */\n  target: TeleportPylonInfo;\n  /** 当前所在塔（玩家 ±60 格内可达且过门的那座；无则 fail=NotNearAPylon 档） */\n  current: TeleportPylonInfo | null;\n  /** 入住城镇 NPC（家 + 现位，tile 单位） */\n  npcs: NpcHomeLike[];\n  /** 目标塔位的群系扫描 */\n  targetScene: SceneFlags;\n  /** 当前塔位的群系扫描（无 current 时可传 EMPTY_SCENE） */\n  currentScene: SceneFlags;\n  downedPlantBoss: boolean;\n}\n\n/** HandleTeleportRequest 门禁五连（:100-205）。传送副作用由调用方执行（落位见文件头）。 */\nexport function checkPylonTeleport(world: World, g: PylonGateInput): PylonTeleportCheck {\n  // 1) 玩家在晶塔旁（:105-109）\n  if (!isPlayerNearPylon(\n    g.current ? [g.current] : [], g.playerTileX, g.playerTileY,\n  ) || g.current == null) {\n    return { ok: false, fail: 'Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecausePlayerIsNotNearAPylon' };\n  }\n  // 2) 目标塔 NPC 足量（:111-118）\n  const needTarget = npcsNeeded(g.target.type);\n  if (!positionHasEnoughNpcs(needTarget, g.target.x, g.target.y, g.npcs)) {\n    return { ok: false, fail: 'Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecauseNotEnoughNPCs' };\n  }\n  // 3) 蜥蜴神庙早入（:120-128）\n  if (!g.downedPlantBoss && isInLihzahrdTempleEarly(world, g.target.x, g.target.y)) {\n    return { ok: false, fail: 'Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecauseAccessingLihzahrdTempleEarly' };\n  }\n  // 4) 目标塔群系要求（:130-136）\n  if (!pylonAcceptsTeleportation(g.target.type, world, g.targetScene, g.target.x, g.target.y)) {\n    return { ok: false, fail: 'Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecauseNotMeetingBiomeRequirements' };\n  }\n  // 5) 当前塔同样过 NPC + 群系门（:138-192；失败文案按最接近档位）\n  const needCurrent = npcsNeeded(g.current.type);\n  const npcOk = positionHasEnoughNpcs(needCurrent, g.current.x, g.current.y, g.npcs);\n  const biomeOk = pylonAcceptsTeleportation(g.current.type, world, g.currentScene, g.current.x, g.current.y);\n  if (!npcOk || !biomeOk) {\n    const fail = !npcOk\n      ? 'Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecauseNotEnoughNPCsAtCurrentPylon'\n      : 'Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecauseNotMeetingBiomeRequirements';\n    return { ok: false, fail };\n  }\n  return { ok: true, fail: null };\n}\n\n/** 地图对焦包围盒（Player.cs:31717-31747）：本塔 + 全部已揭示塔的包围盒外扩 20 格。\n *  IsRevealed（TeleportPylonsMapLayer.cs:138-146）：单人恒揭示（teamBasedSpawnsSeed\n *  专属种分页）→ 全表纳入。返回中心 + 适配缩放（屏宽高 -40 / 盒宽高 的较小值） */\nexport function mapFocusRect(\n  pylons: TeleportPylonInfo[], origin: { x: number; y: number },\n  viewW: number, viewH: number,\n): { cx: number; cy: number; scale: number } {\n  let x0 = origin.x, y0 = origin.y, x1 = origin.x, y1 = origin.y;\n  for (const p of pylons) {\n    if (p.x < x0) x0 = p.x;\n    if (p.y < y0) y0 = p.y;\n    if (p.x > x1) x1 = p.x;\n    if (p.y > y1) y1 = p.y;\n  }\n  x0 -= 20; y0 -= 20; x1 += 20; y1 += 20;   // rect.Inflate(20,20)（:31726）\n  const w = Math.max(1, x1 - x0), h = Math.max(1, y1 - y0);\n  const cx = (x0 + x1) / 2, cy = (y0 + y1) / 2;\n  const scale = Math.min((viewW - 40) / w, (viewH - 40) / h);\n  return { cx, cy, scale };\n}\n"
}

export function isPlayerNearPylon(pylons, ptX, ptY, ptW = 2, ptH = 3): boolean {
  const LX = ptX - 60, HX = ptX + ptW - 1 + 60;
  const LY = ptY - 60, HY = ptY + ptH - 1 + 60;
  return pylons.some(p => p.x + 2 >= LX && p.x <= HX && p.y + 3 >= LY && p.y <= HY);

/** 蜥蜴祭坛右键（Player.cs:32303-32335 1:1）：hardMode+世花已败+无石巨人 →

 *  条件不满足时原版静默（无提示文本）。 */
private useLihzahrdAltar(tx: number, ty: number): void {
  const w = this.world;
  // 条件门（:32307）：!AnyNPCs(245) && hardMode && downedPlantBoss
  if (this.hasNpcId(245) || !w.flags.hardMode || !w.flags['downed_262']) { this.input.rightDown = false; return; }
  // 电池消耗（:32309-32319）：inventory 顺序找 1293 → stack-1 → 空则清格
  const cellId = ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_1293_lihzahrd_power_cell'];
  let consumed = false;
  for (let n = 0; n < this.player.inv.slots.length; n++) {
    const it = this.player.inv.slots[n];
    if (it && it.id === cellId) {
      it.stack--;
      if (it.stack <= 0) this.player.inv.slots[n] = null;
      consumed = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!consumed) { this.input.rightDown = false; return; }
  this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
  this.sfx.playWav? -- check sfx API: this.sfx.play('roar') vs playWav
  this.summonGolemFromAltar();
}

private summonGolemFromAltar(): void {
  const st = this.world.store;
  const pcx = Math.floor(this.player.cx / 16), pcy = Math.floor(this.player.cy / 16);
  // ±20 格找祭坛 237 顶中格（frameX==18 && frameY==0）
  let ax = 0, ay = 0;
  for (let j = pcx - 20; j < pcx + 20; j++) for (let k = pcy - 20; k < pcy + 20; k++) {
    if (st.inBounds(j,k) && st.get(j,k) === ALTAR237 && st.frameX[st.idx(j,k)] === 18 && st.frameY[st.idx(j,k)] === 0) { ax = j; ay = k; }
  }
  if (ax <= 0 || ay <= 0) return;
  let up = ay - 15, down = ay - 15;
  for (let y = ay; y > ay - 50; y--) if (solid) { up = y; break; }
  for (let y = ay; y < ay + 50; y++) if (solid) { down = y; break; }
  const gy = Math.floor((up + up + down) / 3);
  spawn fromVanilla(245, ax*16+8, gy*16) ...
}

```ts
private useEterniaStand(tx: number, ty: number): void {
  const w = this.world; const st = w.store;
  const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
  // num28（:31938-31946）：所点行 frameY 0→+3 / 18→+2 / 36→+1（54→+0）→ 底行
  const fy = st.frameY[i];
  const by = ty + (fy === 0 ? 3 : fy === 18 ? 2 : fy === 36 ? 1 : 0);
  const oa = w.oldOnesArmy;
  if (oa.ongoing) {
    // RequestToSkipWaitTime（:31948-31950 + DD2Event.cs:1794-1810）
    if (requestToSkipWaitTime(oa, this.dd2Host(), tx, ty)) this.sfx.play('killed', 1, tx*16+8, ty*16+8);
    this.input.rightDown = false; return;
  }
  const gates = !this.hasNpcId(548) && w.moonEvent.kind === 0 && this.player.inv.countOf(ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_3828_eternia_crystal']) > 0;
  if (gates && wouldFailSpawningHere(this.dd2Host(), tx, by)) {
    // FailureMessage（DD2Event.cs:615-626，黄 255,255,0）
    this.newText(Lang.text('DungeonDefenders2.BartenderWarning'), 255, 255, 0);
    this.input.rightDown = false; return;
  }
  if (!gates) { this.input.rightDown = false; return; }
  // ConsumeItem(3828)（:31968）
  this.player.inv.remove(ITEM_BY_KEY['vi_3828_eternia_crystal'], 1);
  this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
  summonCrystalDirect(oa, w, this.dd2Host(), tx, by, st.frameX[i], fy);
  // InvasionStart 广播（DD2Event.cs:285，ChatColors.World 50,255,130）
  this.newText(Lang.text('DungeonDefenders2.InvasionStart'), 50, 255, 130);
}

  /** 用原版数据造怪：属性/碰撞/音效全部来自 SetDefaults 提取值 */
  static fromVanilla(id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy | null {
    const v = vanillaNpc(id);
    if (!v) return null;
    const e = new Enemy(`v_${id}`, x, y);
    e.vanillaId = id;
    e.vanilla = v;
    const hit = vanillaSoundName(v.HitSound) ?? 'NPC_Hit_1';
    const kill = vanillaSoundName(v.DeathSound) ?? 'NPC_Killed_1';
    const flying = v.noGravity || v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5 || v.aiStyle === 14;
    e.def = {
      ...e.def,
      // friendly（被缚 NPC 等城镇系）与 critter 一样零接触伤害——原版 friendly 旗
      name: v.name, hp: v.lifeMax, damage: (v.critter || v.friendly) ? 0 : v.damage, defense: v.defense,
      // 原版 knockBackResist 是"承受击退的比例"（0.5=吃一半）；本仓库语义是
      // "抗性"（hurt(): resist<0.9 才生效，kbx*(1-resist)）→ 换算 1-比例
      knockbackResist: Math.max(0, Math.min(0.89, 1 - (v.knockBackResist ?? 0.5))),
      width: Math.round(v.width * (v.scale ?? 1)), height: Math.round(v.height * (v.scale ?? 1)), flying,
      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id),
      nightOnly: v.aiStyle === 2 || v.aiStyle === 5, underground: false,
      mapColor: '#9A8FA0', gore: ['#9A8FA0', '#5E5566', '#C4BACC'],
      hitSound: [hit], killedSound: [kill], drops: [], // 掉落走 NpcDrops 规则树（击杀时求值，不再预展开）
      // 小动物：无接触伤害、不夜行
      ...(v.critter ? { damage: 0, nightOnly: false } : {}),
    };
    e.hp = v.lifeMax;
    e.maxHp = v.lifeMax;
    // 碰撞盒乘 npc.scale(NPC.cs:17747-17748 width/height=(int)(×scale))——
    // 史莱姆王/毁灭者 1.25、血肉墙 1.2:此前未乘,贴图放大了盒子没放大,
    // 判定区比视觉小一圈(与渲染 scale 不一致的"盒贴图不匹配"来源)
    const vScale = v.scale ?? 1;
    e.w = Math.round(v.width * vScale);
    e.h = Math.round(v.height * vScale);
    e.spawnAlpha = v.alpha ?? 0; // 原版 SetDefaults alpha（静态不透明度，NPC.Opacity=1-alpha/255）
    // EoW 族 alpha=255 = 出生全透明渐显标记（其余 alpha 为静态不透明度,勿动）：
    // 钳到 254 并置 alphaFade,由 fixedUpdate 逐 tick 减回 0（原版 AI_006 渐显）
1139:   *  位置按 TryChangingSizeFromBottomCenter（:81836）底边中心锚定改尺寸，
3040:      // slave 挂气球下（cs:43177 nPC.Center = Bottom + (0,-8+56*scale)）

```
  /** 快照到达（Game.netApplyNpcSnaps / netApplyProjSnaps 调用）：设插值目标。
   *  从当前渲染位连续出发（中途到包不跳变）；intervalTicks = 快照周期（15Hz=4 tick） */
  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {
    this.netIx = this.x; this.netIy = this.y;
    this.netAx = x; this.netAy = y;
    this.netIt = 0;
    this.netSnapIvt = Math.max(1, intervalTicks);
  }

  /** 傀儡每 tick 推进：未到目标走 smoothstep 插值（一个快照周期到达），到后纯速度外推 */
  netPuppetStep() {
    if (this.netIt < 1) {
      this.netIt = Math.min(1, this.netIt + 1 / this.netSnapIvt);
      const t = this.netIt * this.netIt * (3 - 2 * this.netIt); // smoothstep
      this.x = this.netIx + (this.netAx - this.netIx) * t;
      this.y = this.netIy + (this.netAy - this.netIy) * t;
      return;
    }
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
  }

  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }

  constructor(root: HTMLElement, cb: GameCallbacks, atlas?: SpriteAtlas | null) {
    this.assets = buildAssets();
    if (atlas) {
      this.atlas = atlas;
      this.autotiler = new AutoTiler(atlas);
    }
    this.renderer = new Renderer(this.assets, atlas);
    this.renderer.attach(root);
    // 音效按需加载（2026-08-10）：不在此预读——播放未命中时后台 fetch+decode、
    // 首播合成兜底；常用战斗/交互声在世界就绪后小集合预热（见 afterWorldLoad），
    // 怪物专属声效随首次受击懒加载
    this.input = new Input(this.renderer.canvas);
    this.cb = cb;
    // 快捷栏数字键：事件驱动（避免单帧内 down+up 被轮询漏掉）
    this.input.keydownHandlers.push((code) => {
      if (this.player && /^Digit[0-9]$/.test(code)) {
        this.player.inv.selected = code === 'Digit0' ? 9 : Number(code.slice(5)) - 1;
        this.cb.onInventoryChanged();
      }
      // 缩放调节
      if (code === 'Equal' || code === 'NumpadAdd') this.setZoom(this.camera?.zoomTarget + 0.15);
      if (code === 'Minus' || code === 'NumpadSubtract') this.setZoom(this.camera?.zoomTarget - 0.15);
      // R:五彩扳手/宏伟蓝图模式循环(红蓝绿黄→剪线→致动器→剪致动器)
      if (code === 'KeyR') {
        const held = this.player?.inv.heldItem();
        if (held && ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.wireTool && (ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.key === 'multicolor_wrench' || ITEM_DEFS[held.id]?.key === 'grand_design')) {
          const modes = [
            [TOOL_RED, TOOL_GREEN, TOOL_BLUE, TOOL_YELLOW],
            [TOOL_CUTTER, TOOL_RED, TOOL_GREEN, TOOL_BLUE, TOOL_YELLOW],
            [TOOL_ACTUATOR],
            [TOOL_CUTTER, TOOL_ACTUATOR],
          ];
          const cur = modes.findIndex((m) => m.reduce((a, b) => a | b, 0) === this.wireToolMode);
          const next = modes[(cur + 1) % modes.length].reduce((a, b) => a | b, 0);
          this.wireToolMode = next;
          const name = next & TOOL_CUTTER
            ? (next & TOOL_ACTUATOR ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Wire.CutActuator') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Wire.Cut'))
            : next & TOOL_ACTUATOR ? Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Wire.Actuator') : Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Wire.All');
          this.cb.onToast(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Wire.ToolMode', name));
        }
      }
    });
  }

  // ================= 生命周期 =================

  async newWorld(seedText: string, width: number, height: number, onProgress?: (label: string, p: number) => void, opts?: { name?: string; evil?: -1 | 0 | 1; onWorldPartial?: (world: import('../world/World').World, passIndex: number, passName: string) => void; /** worker 路径的降采样预览帧回调（位图模式） */ onPreview?: (f: import('../workers/WorldGenClient').PreviewFrame) => void }) {
    this.freshlyCreated = true; // NPC 入驻公告只在新生成的世界播(WorldGen 语义)
    const settleLabel = () => Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.SettleLiquids');
    // ---- worker 路径（generate + settle 一条链在后台完成，UI 全程不卡） ----
    if (!this.genClient) this.genClient = new WorldGenClient();
    if (await this.genClient.probe()) {
      try {
        this.world = await this.genClient.generate(
          { width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, preview: !!opts?.onPreview },
          {
            onPreview: opts?.onPreview,
            // 进度区间映射与主线程路径一致：generate 0–0.7、settle 0.72–0.87
            onProgress: (phase, label, p) => {
              if (phase === 'generate') onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7);
              else onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72 + p * 0.15);
            },
          },
        );
        await this.preloadSceneAssets((label, p) => onProgress?.(label, 0.87 + p * 0.13));
        onProgress?.(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Progress.Done'), 1);
        this.afterWorldLoad();
        this.cb.onWorldReady();
        return;
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof WorldGenUnavailable)) throw e; // 真实业务错误（如 OOM）不吞
        // worker 失败 → 落回主线程路径
      }
    }
    // ---- 主线程 fallback（原路径原样保留：worker 不可用 + 探针依赖） ----
    this.world = await generateWorld({ width, height, seedText, name: opts?.name, evil: opts?.evil, onWorldPartial: opts?.onWorldPartial }, (label, p) => onProgress?.(label, p * 0.7));
    // 水体预沉降：创建世界时全图激活+步进至稳定，带进度条
    onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72);
    await this.settleLiquids((p) => onProgress?.(settleLabel(), 0.72 + p * 0.15));
    // 进图前贴图预载(用户要求:不进图后才动态加载)
    await this.preloadSceneAssets((label, p) => onProgress?.(label, 0.87 + p * 0.13));

betsy.hp / betsy.maxHp : null;\n  }\n\n  /** StartVictoryScene（DD2Event.cs:481-507）：水晶 548 ai[1]=2 / 门 549 撤收 */\n  private startDD2VictoryScene(): void {\n    for (const e of this.entities.enemies as Enemy[]) {\n      if (e.dead) continue;\n      if (e.vanillaId === 548) { e.ai1 = 2; e.ai0 = 2; }\n      else if (e.vanillaId === 549) { e.ai0 = 0; e.ai1 = 1; }\n    }\n  }\n\n  /** UpdateTime（DD2Event.cs:209-251）：每 tick 推进波间间歇/水晶在场判定 */\n  private tickOldOnesArmy(): void {\n    const oa = this.world.oldOnesArmy;\n    if (!oa.ongoing) return;\n    const res = dd2UpdateTime(oa, this.dd2Host(), this.betsyLifeRatio());\n    if (res.stopped) {\n      // 水晶亡 → StopInvasion（:218-220）——败北文案由水晶 AI 演出钩子播\n      return;\n    }\n    if (res.waveAnnounce) {\n      this.newText(invasionWaveText(res.waveAnnounce), 175, 55, 255);\n      if (res.summonBetsy) {\n        const crystal = (this.entities.enemies as Enemy[]).find((e) => !e.dead && e.vanillaId === 548);\n        dd2SummonBetsy(oa, this.dd2Host(), crystal ? crystal.cx : this.player.cx,\n          crystal ? crystal.cy : this.player.cy);\n      }\n    }\n    if (res.lose) {\n      this.newText(Lang.text('DungeonDefenders2.InvasionLose'), 255, 0, 0);\n    }\n  }"

 "new_string": "    ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';\n    ctx.fillStyle = '#C8C0D8';\n    ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n    ctx.fillText(Lang.text('Mods.SandboxWorld.Map.Hint'), viewW / 2, viewH - 16);\n  }\n  private _mapInit = false;\n\n  /** 全屏地图晶塔图标层（TeleportPylonsMapLayer.cs 1:1）：\n   *   - 连线（:52-75）：当前塔（MapPylonTile）→ 其余每座晶塔的黑 6px/白 2px 双线\n   *   - 图标（:77-104）：Extra_182.png 11 帧横排（330×38，帧 30×38），帧号 = 塔型；\n   *     玩家不在晶塔旁 → 半透明灰（color = Gray*0.5 :70-73）\n   *   - 悬停提示（:97-99）：晶塔物品名\n   *  击中判定（Game.tryPylonMapTeleport）：图标中心 = 锚点 + (1.5, 2) 格。 */\n  private drawPylonMapLayer(\n    ctx: CanvasRenderingContext2D, cx0: number, cy0: number,\n    mouseX: number, mouseY: number,\n  ) {\n    if (!this.pylons.length) { this.pylonHoverName = null; return; }\n    const fm = this.fullMap;\n    const icon = this.atlas ? this.atlas.vui('Images_Extra_182.png') : null;\n    // 玩家 ±60 格内无晶塔 → 半透明（IsPlayerNearAPylon :70-73；近似取相机中心玩家位）\n    const p = this._lastPlayer;\n    const dim = p ? !this.pylons.some((py) =>\n      Math.abs(py.x - p.cx / TILE) <= 62 && Math.abs(py.y - p.cy / TILE) <= 63) : true;\n    // 连线（:52-75）\n    if (this.mapPylonTile) {\n      const o = this.mapPylonTile;\n      const ox = cx0 + (o.x + 1.5) * fm.zoom, oy = cy0 + (o.y + 2) * fm.zoom;\n      for (const py of this.pylons) {\n        if (py.x === o.x && py.y === o.y) continue;\n        const tx = cx0 + (py.x + 1.5) * fm.zoom, ty = cy0 + (py.y + 2) * fm.zoom;\n        for (const [c, w] of [['#000000', 6], ['#FFFFFF', 2]] as const) {\n          ctx.strokeStyle = c;\n          ctx.lineWidth = w;\n          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(ox, oy); ctx.lineTo(tx, ty); ctx.stroke();\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    // 图标 + 悬停\n    this.pylonHoverName = null;\n    const FW = 30, FH = 38;   // Extra_182：11 帧 × 30px（330×38）\n    const scale = Math.max(1, Math.min(2, fm.zoom));   // scaleIfSelected 2× / 基线 1×\n    for (const py of this.pylons) {\n      const mx = cx0 + (py.x + 1.5) * fm.zoom, my = cy0 + (py.y + 2) * fm.zoom;\n      const dw = FW * scale, dh = FH * scale;\n      if (mx < -dw || mx > ctx.canvas.width + dw || my < -dh || my > ctx.canvas.height + dh) continue;\n      ctx.globalAlpha = dim ? 0.35 : 1;\n      if (icon) {\n        ctx.drawImage(icon.img, icon.sx + py.type * FW, icon.sy, FW, FH,\n          mx - dw / 2, my - dh / 2, dw, dh);\n      } else {\n        // 贴图未就绪兜底：白描边菱形占位（懒加载次帧补上真图标）\n        ctx.strokeStyle = '#8CF5A9'; ctx.lineWidth = 2;\n        ctx.beginPath();\n        ctx.moveTo(mx, my - dh / 2); ctx.lineTo(mx + dw / 2, my);\n        ctx.lineTo(mx, my + dh / 2); ctx.lineTo(mx - dw / 2, my);\n        ctx.closePath(); ctx.stroke();\n      }\n      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;\n      if (Math.abs(mouseX - mx) <= dw / 2 && Math.abs(mouseY - my) <= dh / 2) {\n        this.pylonHoverName = Lang.itemName(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(py.type));\n      }\n    }\n    if (this.pylonHoverName) {\n      ctx.font = '13px sans-serif';\n      ctx.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF';\n      ctx.textAlign = 'center';\n      ctx.fillText(this.pylonHoverName, mouseX, mouseY - 14);\n    }\n  }\n"
}

function gh(st: { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; w: number; h: number }): number {

{
 "file_path": "~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/BossSummonStations.ts",
 "content": "// Boss 召唤台纯逻辑（右键交互链的位置/行号计算，Player.cs / NPC.cs 1:1）：\n//   - 蜥蜴祭坛 237 → 石巨人 245 的落位（NPC.cs SpawnOnPlayer case 245 :81284-81330）\n//   - 永恒水晶座 466 的\"底行\"回推（Player.cs num28 :31938-31946）\n// Game 侧右键分发（interactAt）调用此处；事件状态机在 world/OldOnesArmy.ts。\nimport type { TileStore } from './TileStore';\n\n/** SpawnOnPlayer case 245（NPC.cs:81284-81330）：玩家 ±20 格内找祭坛顶中格\n *  （frameX==18 && frameY==0——3×2 物件的顶行中列），向上/向下 50 格找实心，\n *  落位 y = (上 + 上 + 下)/3（向上偏置），x = 祭坛列中心。\n *  返回 null = ±20 格内无祭坛（:81308-81311 静默返回）。\n *  @param altarTileId 内部 tile id（TILE_BY_KEY['v_237_lihzahrd_altar']） */\nexport function golemSpawnFromAltar(\n  st: TileStore, altarTileId: number, pcx: number, pcy: number,\n): { x: number; y: number; altarX: number; altarY: number } | null {\n  let ax = 0, ay = 0;\n  for (let j = Math.max(0, pcx - 20); j < Math.min(st.w, pcx + 20); j++) {\n    for (let k = Math.max(0, pcy - 20); k < Math.min(st.h, pcy + 20); k++) {\n      const i = st.idx(j, k);\n      if (st.type[i] !== altarTileId) continue;\n      if (st.frameX[i] === 18 && st.frameY[i] === 0) { ax = j; ay = k; }   // :81300-81304\n    }\n  }\n  if (ax <= 0 || ay <= 0) return null;\n  let up = ay - 15, down = ay - 15;     // num6/num7 初值 = num5-15（:81313-81314）\n  for (let y = ay; y > ay - 50; y--) {  // 向上 50 找实心（:81315-81322）\n    if (st.inBounds(ax, y) && st.isSolid(ax, y)) { up = y; break; }\n  }\n  for (let y = ay; y < ay + 50; y++) {  // 向下 50 找实心（:81323-81330 前段）\n    if (st.inBounds(ax, y) && st.isSolid(ax, y)) { down = y; break; }\n  }\n  const gy = Math.floor((up + up + down) / 3);   // :81324（向上 2:1 偏置）\n  return { x: ax * 16 + 8, y: gy * 16, altarX: ax, altarY: ay };\n}\n\n/** 永恒水晶座所点行 → 底行（Player.cs num28 :31938-31946）：\n *  frameY 0→+3 / 18→+2 / 36→+1 / 54→+0（4 行物件点哪行都归到底行） */\nexport function eterniaStandBottomRow(frameY: number): number {\n  if (frameY === 0) return 3;\n  if (frameY === 18) return 2;\n  if (frameY === 36) return 1;\n  return 0;\n}\n"
}
```

expect(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(0)).toBe(4876);   // Purity\n    expect(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(1)).toBe(4875);   // Jungle\n    expect(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(2)).toBe(4916);   // Hallow\n    expect(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(8)).toBe(4951);   // Victory\n    expect(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(9)).toBe(5652);   // Underworld\n    expect(pylonItemTypeFromStyle(10)).toBe(5653);  // Shimmer\n  });\n\n expect(list[0]).toEqual({ x: 50, y: 60, type: 0 });\n    expect(list[1]).toEqual({ x: 150, y: 80, type: 8 });\n  });\n\n  it('pylonAnchorAt：点击任意格回推 3×4 锚点（Player.cs:31713-31715）', () => {\n    const w expect(npcsNeeded(PYLON.Jungle)).toBe(2);\n    const near: NpcHomeLike[] = [\n      { homeX: 100, homeY: 100, x: 100, y: 101 },\n      { homeX: 102, homeY: 100, x: 102, y: 101 },\n    ];\n    expect(positionHasEnoughNpcs(2, 100, 100, near)).toBe(true);\n    // 家在 169×124 窗外不计（:236-239）\n    expect(positionHasEnoughNpcs(2, 100, 100, [\n      near[0], { homeX: 300, homeY: 100, x: 300, y: 101 },\n    ])).toBe(false);\n    // NPC 现位距家 ≥100 格不计（:240-243）\n    expect(positionHasEnoughNpcs(2, 100, 100, [\n      near[0], { homeX: 101, homeY: 100, x: 250, y: 100 },\n    ])).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('群系要求（DoesPylonAcceptTeleportation :254-312）', () => {\n    const w = makeWorld(300, 400);\n    w.groundLevel = 50;\n    const pure = { ...EMPTY_SCENE };\n expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.SurfacePurity, w, pure, 100, 60)).toBe(false);\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.SurfacePurity, w, { ...EMPTY_SCENE, zoneJungle: true }, 100, expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Jungle, w, pure, 100, 100)).toBe(false);\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Hallow, w, { ...EMPTY_SCENE, zoneHallow: true }, 100, 100)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Snow, w, { ...EMPTY_SCENE, zoneSnow: true }, 100, 100)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Desert, w, { ...EMPTY_SCENE, zoneDesert: true }, 100, 100)).toBe(true);\n    // Victory 恒真（:300）；地下塔 worldSurface 含（:298）\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Victory, w, pure, 100, 300)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Underground, w, pure, 100, 50)).toBe(true);\n    expect(pylonAcceptsTeleportation(PYLON.Underground, w, pure, 100, 49)).toBe(false);\n    // 地狱塔 = expect(checkPylonTeleport(w, base).ok).toBe(true);\n    // 1) 无当前塔（玩家不在任何晶塔 ±60 格内）→ NotNearAPylon（:105-109）\n    expect(checkPylonTeleport(w, { ...base, current: null }).fail)\n for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) w2.store.setWall(200 + dx, 100, 87);\n    expect(checkPylonTeleport(w2, {\n      ...base, downedPlantBoss: false, target: { x: 200, y: 100, type: PYLON.Victory },\n y: 40 },\n    ];\n    expect(checkPylonTeleport(w, {\n      ...base, npcs: housed, target: { x: 200, y: 100, type: PYLON.SurfacePurity },\n }).fail).toBe('Net.CannotTeleportToPylonBecauseNotEnoughNPCsAtCurrentPylon');\n  });\n\n  it('地图对焦（Player.cs:31717-31747）：包围盒外扩 20 + (屏-40)/盒 取小', () => {\n    const pylons: TeleportPylonInfo[] = [\n private consumeVanillaItem(vid: number): boolean {
  const slots = this.player.inv.slots;
  for (let n = 0; n < slots.length; n++) {
    const it = slots[n];
    if (it && slotVanillaId(it.id) === vid) {
      it.stack--;
      if (it.stack <= 0) slots[n] = null;
      this.cb.onInventoryChanged?.();
      return true;

  }
  return false;
}

}

 "new_string": "  /** 晶塔表刷新（放置/破坏晶塔与右键时调用） */\n  refreshPylons(): void {\n    this.pylons = scanPylons(this.world);\n    this.renderer.pylons = this.pylons;\n  }\n\n  /** 晶塔整件破碎（TETeleportationPylon.Framing_CheckTile :77-125）：\n   *  按帧回推 3×4 锚点 → 清整塔 → 掉对应样式的放置物品（GetPylonItemTypeFromTileStyle） */\n  private breakPylon(x: number, y: number): void {\n    const st = this.world.store;\n    const anchor = pylonAnchorAt(this.world, x, y);\n    if (!anchor) { st.setTile(x, y, 0); return; }\n    const i0 = st.idx(x, y);\n    const style = pylonStyleFromFrame(st.frameX[i0]);\n    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {\n      for (let dy = 0; dy < 4; dy++) {\n        if (st.inBounds(anchor.x + dx, anchor.y + dy)\n          && st.get(anchor.x + dx, anchor.y + dy) === TILE_BY_KEY['v_597_pylons']) {\n          st.setTile(anchor.x + dx, anchor.y + dy, 0);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    this.chunks.markDirtyArea(anchor.x - 1, anchor.y - 1, anchor.x + 3, anchor.y + 4);\n    const key = VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[pylonItemTypeFromStyle(style)];\n    if (key) this.spawnDrop(anchor.x * TILE + 24, anchor.y * TILE, key, 1, 0, -2);\n    this.sfx.play('place');\n    this.refreshPylons();\n  }"
}
```

Looking at the code: `_mapClickLatch` gates the minimap-open branch; setting true then cleared when mouse released. Since we opened map via right-click (not left on minimap), setting the latch avoids the left-click-open branch re-triggering while button held. But rightDown is separate from mouseDown... The left-click open branch requires `inp.mouseDown && !this._mapClickLatch`. If user right-clicks (mouseDown for right button? does inp.mouseDown track left only?) — likely left only. Setting latch is harmless.

Also `_mapOpenClick = true` prevents the same-frame drag. Fine.

2. `tryPylonMapTeleport` — pylons list may be stale if user placed pylons then opened map via minimap (not via pylon right-click): mapPylonTile null → returns false → falls back to arbitrary teleport. Vanilla: clicking pylon icons on the map works whenever map open? In vanilla the pylon icons are drawn always (map layer), and clicking any icon requests teleport — but the gates require player near a pylon anyway. Hmm: in vanilla, opening map normally (M key) still shows pylon icons and clicking teleports (HandleTeleportRequest checks IsPlayerNearAPylon). So my gating on mapPylonTile != null is more restrictive than vanilla. Improve: in the map-click handler, refresh pylons when map opens (minimap click path) so icons show; and allow tryPylonMapTeleport whenever pylons exist and player is near one.

Let me restructure: in tryPylonMapTeleport, drop the mapPylonTile-null early return for the icon hit; but keep "current pylon != hit pylon" check? Vanilla allows clicking ANY pylon icon including the one you're standing at? It would teleport you to the same spot (no-op-ish). Vanilla doesn't exclude the origin (the layer only draws lines from MapPylonTile, but click works for all). Also the origin anchor: if map opened via minimap, mapPylonTile is -1 → lines not drawn. Fine.

Also need pylons refreshed when map opens via minimap: add refreshPylons() in the minimap-open branch. And renderer.pylons updated there too (refreshPylons does that).

But careful: refreshPylons scans whole world (5M cells) — on map open that's a one-off ~10ms. Acceptable.

Also `if (hit.x === this.mapPylonTile.x ...)` guard should become: skip if hit is the pylon the player is standing at? Vanilla: clicking the pylon you're next to teleports you to itself — allowed but pointless. Keep guard only when mapPylonTile set. Simplify: remove that guard entirely (vanilla doesn't have it).

key = num switch { 1 => NotEnoughNPCsAtCurrentPylon, 2 => NotMeetingBiomeRequirements, _ => NotNearAPylon }

where num tracks the furthest stage passed: 1 = found pylon in range; 2 = also NPCs ok; then biome check per pylon. My simplification: if current exists but NPC fails → AtCurrentPylon; else if biome fails → BiomeRequirements. Reasonably faithful (documented as the two-tier selection). Fine.
